# Asset manifest

Every file shipped under `public/`: where it comes from, who made it, how it was processed and what it
does in the piece. Third-party sources are CC0 or public domain, except the Rocketbox characters (MIT), the fonts (OFL, Apache 2.0)
and the models under CC BY; the required notices are in [THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md](THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md).
The models, materials and HDRIs are generated by `npm run assets` from
[`scripts/assets.json`](scripts/assets.json) (see the README); the rooms' scans and props are converted or made first by
`scripts/build-scans.mjs` and `scripts/build-props.mjs`, and the rooms' sounds are built by `scripts/build-room-audio.py`.

Sizes are those of the shipped files (1 KiB = 1024 bytes). Triangle counts are after optimization.

| Kind | Files | Shipped size |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| 3D models | 229 | 39.02 MiB |
| PBR materials | 129 | 22.74 MiB |
| HDRIs | 4 | 5.35 MiB |
| Audio | 660 | 46.18 MiB |
| Fonts and their licenses | 15 | 0.51 MiB |
| **Total** | **1037** | **113.80 MiB** |

## 3D models

Source: [Poly Haven](https://polyhaven.com), license [CC0 1.0](https://polyhaven.com/license).
Each model is the Poly Haven **1k glTF**, then processed by `scripts/build-assets.mjs`: dedup, prune,
weld, keyframe resample, textures re-encoded as **WebP (quality 82)** at most the listed size,
**meshopt** compression (`EXT_meshopt_compression` + `KHR_mesh_quantization`). Where noted, meshes are
simplified with the meshoptimizer simplifier: *ratio* is the target fraction of indices kept, *error*
the largest allowed deviation as a fraction of the mesh radius (pipeline default 0.002).

## Scans and other 3D sources

Converted by `scripts/build-scans.mjs` (decimated where needed, stood upright at the origin, 1 m tall), then optimized by
`scripts/build-assets.mjs`. The licence of each was read at its source; the CC BY ones are credited in
[THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md](THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md) and in the piece's menu.

## Models for the rooms

The models the rooms of the seven evenings load (each room lists them in `assets.models`, see `src/experience/rooms/list/`).
Poly Haven models are processed as under [3D models](#3d-models). The others are converted by `scripts/build-scans.mjs` (stood upright,
in their real size, given a look and UVs in meters where noted) or made by `scripts/build-props.mjs`, then optimized by
`scripts/build-assets.mjs`. The CC BY ones are credited in [THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md](THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md) and in the piece's menu.

**Licences, read at the source on 2026-11-05.** Poly Haven: polyhaven.com/license, all assets CC0. ambientCG: docs.ambientcg.com/license, CC0 1.0. The Base Mesh: thebasemesh.com/faq and its home page, all models CC0. Poly Pizza: the Licence field on each model's own page, stored per model. OpenGameArt: the License(s) field on the spider's page, CC-BY 3.0.

**The dogs and the shark.** All four source pages (poly.pizza/m/wcWiuEqwzq, /m/P1gU3Qkr9r, /m/y4wdQpg767, /m/AyHTK3zUSG, by Quaternius) read "Public Domain (CC0)", their stored record "CC0 1.0", with no added terms. Quaternius's own page for his animated animals pack (quaternius.com/packs/ultimateanimatedanimals.html) gives CC0, though it does not name the animals. CC0 asks for no credit; Quaternius is thanked in THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md all the same.

**The spider's chain.** Its page says p0ss keeps sunburn's CC BY 3.0 and asks that sunburn be credited (done in THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md and in the menu); sunburn's own page offers CC-BY 3.0 or GPL 3.0. Its page also says the hair in its texture comes from p0ss's own Woodland Animals Texture Pack (opengameart.org/content/woodland-animals-texture-pack), listed there as CC-BY-SA 3.0 and GPL 2.0, from a photograph by Michelle Buntin on burningwell.org, a repository of public domain images. So nothing upstream restricts what p0ss released here as CC BY 3.0, his own work over public domain sources.

## PBR materials

Sources: [Poly Haven](https://polyhaven.com) ([CC0 1.0](https://polyhaven.com/license)) and
[ambientCG](https://ambientcg.com) ([CC0 1.0](https://docs.ambientcg.com/license/)).
Every material ships as three WebP images: `textures/<key>_color.webp` (sRGB, quality 84),
`textures/<key>_normal.webp` (OpenGL convention, quality 90) and `textures/<key>_orm.webp`
(R = ambient occlusion, G = roughness, B = metalness, quality 88). Poly Haven materials use their packed
`arm` map as ORM; ambientCG maps are packed by the pipeline, and a missing map gets a neutral value
(the paving stones have no metalness map, so B = 0).

## Character and animations

Source: [Microsoft Rocketbox](https://github.com/microsoft/Microsoft-Rocketbox), license MIT,
Copyright (c) 2020 Microsoft. The full license text is reproduced in
[THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md](THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md), as the MIT license requires.

Conversion (`scripts/character/`): `server.mjs` downloads the avatar FBX, its seven TGA maps and the
animation FBX files from the repository into `.asset-cache/rocketbox/`, and serves `convert.html`, which
runs three.js `FBXLoader` → `GLTFExporter` in a real browser. The converter scales centimeters to
meters, turns the materials into PBR (roughness derived from the specular maps), makes the hair cards
alpha-tested and downsizes every map to 1024. The result, `.asset-cache/character/photographer.glb`, then
goes through `npm run assets` like every other model (`source: "local"`, WebP 1024, meshopt).

| Asset (key / file) | Source asset | Source | URL | License | Version / resolution used | Size | Purpose in the piece |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| `photographer` / `models/photographer.glb` | Avatar `Male_Adult_07` (`Export/Male_Adult_07.fbx`, textures `m013_*`: body and head color, normal, specular; opacity) | Microsoft Rocketbox | [github.com/microsoft/Microsoft-Rocketbox](https://github.com/microsoft/Microsoft-Rocketbox) (`Assets/Avatars/Adults/Male_Adult_07`) | MIT | Exported FBX mesh, 1 skin; 7 maps → WebP 1024; meshopt; 7.6k tris; both clips below in the same file | 956 KiB | The photographer. Also cloned as the shadow-only double frozen at the shutter (the shadow that stays inside the picture) as the small figure at the far end of the alley, aiming a camera back at you (it backs away into the giant print), and as the photographer standing on the sea who takes the last picture. |
| `m_idle_breathe_01` (clip in `photographer.glb`) | Animation `m_idle_breathe_01` | Microsoft Rocketbox | [github.com/microsoft/Microsoft-Rocketbox](https://github.com/microsoft/Microsoft-Rocketbox) (`Assets/Animations/all_animations_max_motextr_static`) | MIT | Whole clip, 2.73 s, keyframes resampled | (included) | Breathing, layered additively on every pose. |
| `m_take_picture` (clip in `photographer.glb`) | Animation `m_take_picture` | Microsoft Rocketbox | same as above | MIT | Whole clip, 8.50 s, keyframes resampled | (included) | The performance: scrubbed by the timeline (raise, eye, lower, look), with IK on the left arm and hand for the print hand-off. |

### The rooms

Source: [Freesound](https://freesound.org), each sound under [CC0 1.0](http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/) (checked on each sound's own page, and on its uploader's profile for added terms, on 2026-11-05), and sound made for this piece. The files are cut from Freesound's public HQ MP3 previews; every cut, filter and synthesis is in `scripts/build-room-audio.py`. Every file is optional: a file that fails to load leaves silence.

## Credit requests

CC0 requires no attribution; these are courtesies to honor in the notices, as the rest of the manifest already does:

- **232148, Ring Around the Rosie, Child Vocal (RaspberryTickle)**: the uploader politely asks for credit on all her sounds.
- **367679, Horror Scream Echo (FunWithSound)**: the page invites credit.
- **In the rooms**: 20108 (air over mic.wav, by cognito perceptu: his profile: he likes to be credited as cognito perceptu); 79382 (boxofscrews.wav, by j1987: credit welcomed); 185059 (Deafening Whispers, by unfa: if credited: Tobiasz 'unfa' Karoń, with a link to the sound); 202612 (FXLM neon lights flicker bathroom.wav, by LudwigMueller: credit welcomed); 209014 (Ceramic Tink, by OwlStorm: no credit needed; if given, Ashe Kirk or Owlish Media); 215385 (porcelain clinks slides.wav, by Reitanna: the profile recommends a credit, as Reitanna Seishin); 216606 (Strange Cloth Rubbing, by unfa: if credited: Tobiasz 'unfa' Karoń, with a link to the sound); 219677 (Wooden Chair Move.mp3, by harrietniamh: the profile asks for a credit when a sound is used in a production); 242867 (blowing out candle.wav, by Reitanna: the profile recommends a credit, as Reitanna Seishin); 273150 (Heart Beating, by michorvath: credit welcomed); 366159 (Underwater [Loop] AMB.wav, by DCSFX: credit optional); 376644 (Demential, strong wind through the edges of an aluminum door 1 @ 96 KHz 24bit, by Bronxio: credit optional, welcomed); 456037 (heavy breathing while running, by florianreichelt: the profile asks for a tag of instagram.com/florianreichelt in the description); 456433 (Old Rotary Phone Ringing - Western Electric Bell 500 phone, by mycompasstv: the profile: credit much appreciated, do not re-post the sound or claim it as your own); 459970 (Footsteps on hollow wood, by florianreichelt: the profile asks for a tag of instagram.com/florianreichelt in the description); 556722 (Record Player Needle is dropped & Vinyl crackling, by Breviceps: links to the work welcomed); 587442 (Dropping sheet metal, by SamsterBirdies: credit welcomed); 587443 (Scrap metal dropping / crashing, by SamsterBirdies: credit welcomed); 588400 (Concert Hall Silence Ambiance.mp3, by FunWithSound: credit welcomed); 706430 (swiming pool underwater.wav, by bruno.auzet: the profile asks you to think about crediting freesound.org); 786515 (ELECBuzz_An Incandescent lamp.The Last seconds.Unstable Buzzing_EM, by newlocknew: the profile asks for the nickname and the file name in the credits); 794817 (Fireplace Rumble, by SamsterBirdies: credit welcomed); 800114 (quartz crystal tapping ceramic plate-003.wav, by CVLTIV8R: credit welcomed); 800117 (shells and quartz crystals rustling and scraping on marble.wav, by CVLTIV8R: credit welcomed); 859154 (Loud Bang Crashing Metal Chunk Dropped in Metal Dumpster, by qubodup: CC0 on its page, while its signature points to the CC BY credit on the profile: name qubodup under sounds, so that either reading is met); 861352 (Annoying Voiceover Wet Mouth Clicks, by qubodup: CC0 on its page, while its signature points to the CC BY credit on the profile: name qubodup under sounds, so that either reading is met).

CC0 needs no credit; these are courtesies. Some uploaders only ask to hear where a sound went, by a comment on its page (it takes a Freesound login, so nobody has done it yet): 828623 (c64cosmin), 417537 (danthaiwang), 320913 (mickdow), 634746 (TravieDoodle), 696021 (Funky_Audio), 547976 (trijohnstone), 524807 (SamuelGremaud), 172411 (EagleStealthTeam), 459970 (florianreichelt), 574752 (ericnorcross81), 418487 (dersinnsspace), 719430 (BaggoNotes). felix.blume (684518, 447375, 639332, 217884, 146436) and klankbeeld (611197) invite comments and ratings. Worth keeping a copy of the page: 639332 and 509477 mention faint voices or sparse people outside the stretches used (the spans used were checked by spectrogram, not by ear); 848923 says it was composed of its uploader's recordings in a shopping mall, pitched down, with reverb; 451914 mentions distant noises, of which only the bass under 350 Hz is used; 185059 (whisper_gabble): its uploader does not consent to training generative models; playing it is fine, do not feed it to any such pipeline. RutgerMuller (51153, 104013, 104016, 104019, 104351, 484307) asks that his unedited sounds are not sold on, and that a credit does not read as a collaboration: everything here is cut and filtered.

## Notes for the record

- Nothing here is on the rejected list (50750, 127967, 69979, 585264, 613091, 347836), and 179651 is not shipped. All music box recordings being rejected, the tune is synthesized from a score of my own in `audio.config.ts`.
- 185059 (`wall`): its uploader does not consent to his sounds training generative models. Playing it is fine; do not feed it to any ML pipeline.
- 211621 (`tear`): its CC0 is very recent (the author's description dates it two days before the check): keep a snapshot of its page as evidence.
- 329866 (`vocal`): made from a friend's laughter over Skype, heavily processed; the uploader grants free use, the friend's consent is not stated (kept low in the mix, unrecognizable).
- 273804 (`choir`): carries a field-recording tag and a comment about birds; the file is low-passed at 2.2 kHz, under the doubtful tonal peaks at 2.5–2.9 kHz.
- The rooms' sounds: also on the rejected list, though their Freesound field says CC0: 353159, 370935, 656033, 670070 (Kinoton: each page says that using the sound is agreeing to a license agreement, and his profile's terms of use forbid making the raw files available to a third party); 646874 (Andron827: the profile adds a you may not, and contact me for commercial use). Unclear terms, so not used. Also read and turned down on the same reading: 842778 (cesar-gutierrez-99: the signature asks for a credit and says not for AI); 805676 (giddster: the profile asks to be credited, with a link); 618352, 618353, 618354, 678673, 679047, 679048 (tferrino: each page says the raw file may not be put online or re-distributed); 122183 (qubodup: the signature points to CC BY requirements); 196619 and 89448 (built from other people's sounds, not all named); 177007 (built partly from purchased effects); 398013 (recorded off a storm simulator).
- The rooms' models: turned down on the way, the dog statue and the shark of the game 0 A.D. on Wikimedia Commons, which are CC BY-SA 3.0.

## Fonts

Source: [Google Fonts](https://fonts.google.com), the WOFF2 subsets its CSS API serves (`latin` and `latin-ext`: Ş ş Ğ ğ İ exist only
in `latin-ext`, ı Ç ç Ö ö Ü ü only in `latin`, so Turkish needs both), self-hosted unmodified in `public/fonts/` with their licenses,
copied verbatim from the [google/fonts](https://github.com/google/fonts) repository (see [THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md](THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)).
Every file below was downloaded again from the listed URL on 2026-11-04 and compared byte for byte with the shipped one.

Latin and latin-ext subsets of five typefaces are self-hosted in `public/fonts/` (WOFF2, from Google Fonts) and ship with the build, each
next to its license:

| Typeface | Files | Designer | License | License file |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Anton | `anton-400{,-latin-ext}.woff2` | Vernon Adams | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | `public/fonts/OFL-Anton.txt` |
| Special Elite | `special-elite-400{,-latin-ext}.woff2` | Brian J. Bonislawsky (Astigmatic) | Apache License 2.0 | `public/fonts/LICENSE-SpecialElite.txt` |
| Courier Prime | `courier-prime-400{,-latin-ext}.woff2` | Alan Dague-Greene | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | `public/fonts/OFL-CourierPrime.txt` |
| Protest Revolution | `protest-revolution-400{,-latin-ext}.woff2` | Octavio Pardo | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | `public/fonts/OFL-ProtestRevolution.txt` |
| Just Me Again Down Here | `just-me-again-down-here-400{,-latin-ext}.woff2` | Kimberly Geswein | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | `public/fonts/OFL-JustMeAgainDownHere.txt` |

The fonts are unmodified apart from Google Fonts' subsetting; none of the license headers declares a Reserved Font Name. Copyright notices, as they

## Made for this piece

Built in code for this project, with no third-party assets; listed so that nothing on screen is
unaccounted for.

| Element | Where | How it is made |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Instant camera | `src/experience/camera-object/InstantCamera.ts` | A generic, unbranded instant camera modeled procedurally: rounded charcoal body, leatherette band, lathe-turned lens barrel with knurl and a glass element, viewfinder, flash window, shutter button, film slot and strap lugs. Its plastic and leatherette grain are bump maps generated on a canvas. |
| Instant print | `src/experience/photograph/Photograph.ts` | Paper body with thickness and a vertex-shader curl, paper fibre texture generated on a canvas, and an image shader that develops out of a murky green-brown, carries film grain and a glossy sheen, and can become a live window into the world during the transition. The aged variant (the worn print taped to the utility cabinet inside the picture) adds yellowing, stains and a handwritten date in Homemade Apple. |
| The photograph's image | `src/experience/Experience.ts`, `src/experience/transition/PhotoPortal.ts` | Rendered at the shutter from the lens, out of the same scene (1024 × 1024 half-float target). |
| Overhead cables | `src/experience/world/Alley.ts` (`buildCables`) | Sagging spans between the facades (a parabolic catenary approximation), swept as thin tubes. |
| Sky dome | `src/experience/world/SkyDome.ts` | A back-faced sphere drawn on the far plane; its shader samples the real and the dream HDRIs and blends between them, tilting and turning the dream sky. |
| Lit windows | `src/experience/world/Alley.ts` (`buildLights`) | Emissive room planes behind a few windows, each with a point light that spills into the alley. |
| Curtain | `src/experience/world/Alley.ts` (`buildCurtain`) | A sheer, half-drawn curtain in one lit room: a subdivided plane whose folds and draught are a vertex-shader displacement. |
| Corridor behind the giant door | `src/experience/director/DreamDirector.ts` (`buildCorridor`) | Inside the picture only: a dark box seen through the oversized doorway, its ceiling panels receding through the fog: more inside than any building could hold. |
| Building mass | `src/experience/world/FacadeBuilder.ts` | Dark boxes behind the facades so that windows look into interiors rather than through the buildings. |
| Window onto the real alley | `src/experience/director/DreamDirector.ts` | Inside the picture one window shows a live render of the real alley from the opening shot. |
| Post-processing and grade | `src/experience/render/` | Depth of field, affine color grades, AgX tone mapping, grain, vignette and paper sheen in one composite pass. |
| Sound | `src/experience/audio/AudioEngine.ts` | The near-silent tonal bed, and every sound that has no recorded file listed under [Audio](#audio), is synthesized with the Web Audio API. |
| Dust sheets and a shroud | `models/sheet_chair.glb`, `models/sheet_armchair.glb`, `models/sheet_sofa.glb`, `models/sheet_table.glb`, `models/sheet_lamp.glb`, `models/sheet_figure.glb`, `models/shroud_body.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | A cloth simulation run in the script over boxes and capsules that are then thrown away; the result is baked. 37533 triangles, 262 KiB. |
| Iron bed with a sheet that sits up | `models/bed_iron.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Frame swept from tubes; the sheet comes from the same cloth simulation, run twice, its second state stored as a morph target. 3560 triangles, 39 KiB. |
| Eye in a knot | `models/eyeball.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Sphere, lids and knot from code; iris and veins drawn in code. 1952 triangles, 40 KiB. |
| Paper party hat | `models/party_hat.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | A cone and a pompom from code, stripes drawn in code. 192 triangles, 6 KiB. |
| Balloon | `models/balloon.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Lathe and string from code. 804 triangles, 8 KiB. |
| Share image | `og.jpg` | The title screen itself, captured at 1200 × 630 with its buttons hidden: the title and the motto broken off, in the piece's own fonts (OFL). 124 KiB. |
| Birthday candle | `models/candle_party.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Lathe and flame from code, stripes drawn in code. 98 triangles, 7 KiB. |
| Music box | `models/music_box.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Boxes, comb, cylinder and crank from code. 422 triangles, 11 KiB. |
| Dental handpiece | `models/dental_drill.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Lathe and swept hose from code. 444 triangles, 6 KiB. |
| Turkish tea glass | `models/tea_glass.glb` (`scripts/build-props.mjs`) | Lathe from code: waisted glass, tea, saucer. 636 triangles, 9 KiB. |
